            return;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; ++i) {

        }
    }
}

```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}

public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```java
public class MatrixSum {
    public static int[][] matrixSum(int[][] a, int[][] b) {
        int n = a.length;
        int m = a[0].length;

        int[][] c = new int[n][m];

        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < m; j++) {
                c[i][j] = a[i][j] + b[i][j];
            }
        }

        return c;
    }
}

```

    public static int[][] matrixSum(int[][] a, int[][] b) {
        int n = a.length;
        int m = a[0].length;

        int[][] c = new int[n][m];

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; (\forall int y; 0 <= y < m; c[x][y] == a[x][y] + b[x][y]));
        //@ loop_writes c[*][*], i;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 0 <= j <= m;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; 0 < y < j; c[i][y] == a[i][y] + b[i][y]);  
            //@ loop_writes c[i][*], j;
            //@ decreases m - j;

                //@ requires a[i][j] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE - b[i][j];
                c[i][j] = a[i][j] + b[i][j];
            }
        }

        return c;
    }

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; (\forall int y; 0 < y <= m; c[x][y] == a[x][y] + b[x][y]));  
        //@ loop_writes c[*][*], i;

                //@ requires a[i][j] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE - b[i][j];

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 < x < i; (\exists int y; 0 - 1 <= y < m; c[x][y] == a[x][y] + b[x][y]));

                //@ requires a[i][j] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE - b[i][j];

            }
        }

        return c;
    }

    public static int[][] matrixSum(int[][] a, int[][] b) {

        int m = a[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
 
        //@ loop_writes c[*][*], i;

            //@ maintaining 0 <= j <= m;

                //@ requires a[i][j] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE - b[i][j];

    public static int[][] matrixSum(int[][] a, int[][] b) {

        int m = a[0].length;

        int[][] c = new int[n][m];

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
 
        //@ loop_writes c[*][*], i;

    //@ requires a.length > 0 && b.length > 0;
    //@ requires a[0] != null && b[0] != null; 
    //@ requires a.length == b.length && a[0].length > 0 && a[0].length == b[0].length; 
    //@ requires (\forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; (\forall int j; 0 <= j < a[0].length; a[i][j] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE - b[i][j])); 
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result != null;
    //@ ensures \result.length == a.length && \result[0].length == a[0].length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 < i < a.length; (\exists int j; 0 < j <= a[0].length; \result[i][j] == a[i][j] + b[i][j]));  
    public static int[][] matrixSum(int[][] a, int[][] b) {
        int n = a.length;
        int m = a[0].length;

        int[][] c = new int[n][m];

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
 
        //@ loop_writes c[*][*], i;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 0 <= j <= m;
 
            //@ loop_writes c[i][*], j;
            //@ decreases m - j;
            for (int j = 0; j < m; j++) {
                //@ requires a[i][j] <= Integer.MAX_VALUE - b[i][j];
                c[i][j] = a[i][j] + b[i][j];
            }
        }

        return c;
    }
}

    //@ requires a.length > 0 && b.length > 0;
    //@ requires a[0] != null && b[0] != null;

    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result != null;
    //@ ensures \result.length == a.length && \result[0].length == a[0].length;
    //@ ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i < a.length; (\exists int j; 0 < j < a[0].length; \result[i][j] == a[i][j] + b[i][j]));  
    public static int[][] matrixSum(int[][] a, int[][] b) {
        int n = a.length;
        int m = a[0].length;

        int[][] c = new int[n][m];

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= n;
 
        //@ loop_writes c[*][*], i;

            //@ maintaining 0 <= j <= m;
 
            //@ loop_writes c[i][*], j;

            }
        }

        return c;
    }
